00064:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) and v > 0 for v in size)
00065:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and 0 < radius <= min(size)/2
00066:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) for v in center)
00067:     return fillet_box(size, radius=radius, center=center, color=color)
00068: 
00069: def line(radius, start, end, color):
00070:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0
00071:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) for v in (*start,*end))
00072:     assert sum((a-b)**2 for a,b in zip(start,end)) > 1e-16
00073:     return rod(radius, start=start, end=end, color=color)
00074: 
00075: def tube(points, radius, color):
00076:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0
00077:     assert len(points) >= 2
00078:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) for p in points for v in p)
00079:     assert all(sum((a-b)**2 for a,b in zip(p,q)) > 1e-16
00080:                for p,q in zip(points,points[1:]))
00081:     return sweep_tube(points=points, radius=radius, color=color)
00082: 
00083: def sphere(radius, center, color):
00084:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0
00085:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) for v in center)
00086:     return ball(radius, center=center, color=color)

00291: def subtree_meshes(parent_name):
00292:     parent = bpy.data.objects.get(parent_name)
00293:     assert parent is not None, parent_name
00294:     stack,meshes = [parent],[]
00295:     while stack:
00296:         obj = stack.pop()
00297:         if obj.type == 'MESH':
00298:             meshes.append(obj)
00299:         stack.extend(list(obj.children))
00300:     assert meshes, parent_name
00301:     return meshes
00302: 
00303: def local_roughness(parent_name,value):
00304:     copies = {}
00305:     for obj in subtree_meshes(parent_name):
00306:         for slot in obj.material_slots:
00307:             original = slot.material
00308:             if original is None:
00309:                 continue
00310:             key = original.as_pointer()
00311:             if key not in copies:
00312:                 mat = original.copy()
00313:                 mat.name = f'{parent_name} / {original.name}'
00314:                 mat.use_nodes = True
00315:                 bsdf = mat.node_tree.nodes.get('Principled BSDF')
00316:                 if bsdf is not None:
00317:                     bsdf.inputs['Roughness'].default_value = value
00318:                 copies[key] = mat
00319:             slot.material = copies[key]

00063: def finite_vector(v):
00064:     return all(math.isfinite(float(n)) for n in v)
00065: 
00066: def attach(parent, name, node):
00067:     assert name not in parent.members, f'Duplicate include key: {name}'
00068:     return parent.include(name, node)
00069: 
00070: def put(parent, name, size, center, color):
00071:     assert finite_vector(size) and all(n > 0 for n in size)
00072:     assert finite_vector(center)
00073:     return attach(parent, name, box(size=size, center=center, color=color))
00074: 
00075: def tube(parent, name, a, b, radius=.022, color=IRON):
00076:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0
00077:     assert finite_vector(a) and finite_vector(b)
00078:     assert sum((a[i] - b[i]) ** 2 for i in range(3)) > 1e-12
00079:     return attach(parent, name, rod(radius=radius, start=a, end=b, color=color))
00080: 
00081: def sphere(parent, name, radius, center, color):
00082:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0 and finite_vector(center)
00083:     return attach(parent, name, ball(radius=radius, center=center, color=color))

00348: def isolated_materials(parent_name):
00349:     parent = bpy.data.objects.get(parent_name)
00350:     assert parent is not None, f'Missing parent: {parent_name}'
00351:     meshes = [obj for obj in parent.children_recursive if obj.type == 'MESH']
00352:     assert meshes, f'Empty mesh subtree: {parent_name}'
00353:     result = []
00354:     for obj in meshes:
00355:         assert len(obj.material_slots) > 0
00356:         for slot in obj.material_slots:
00357:             assert slot.material is not None
00358:             slot.material = slot.material.copy()
00359:             slot.material.use_nodes = True
00360:             bsdf = slot.material.node_tree.nodes.get('Principled BSDF')
00361:             assert bsdf is not None
00362:             result.append(bsdf)
00363:     return result

00048: # Helpers audit dimensions and prevent silent include replacement.
00049: def finite_vector(v):
00050:     return all(math.isfinite(float(n)) for n in v)
00051: 
00052: def attach(parent, name, node):
00053:     assert name not in parent.members, f'Duplicate include key: {name}'
00054:     return parent.include(name, node)
00055: 
00056: def put(parent, name, size, center, color):
00057:     assert finite_vector(size) and all(n > 0 for n in size)
00058:     assert finite_vector(center)
00059:     return attach(parent, name, box(size=size, center=center, color=color))
00060: 
00061: def tube(parent, name, a, b, radius=.022, color=IRON):
00062:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0
00063:     assert finite_vector(a) and finite_vector(b)
00064:     assert sum((a[i] - b[i]) ** 2 for i in range(3)) > 1e-12
00065:     return attach(parent, name, rod(radius=radius, start=a, end=b, color=color))
00066: 
00067: def sphere(parent, name, radius, center, color):
00068:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0
00069:     assert finite_vector(center)
00070:     return attach(parent, name, ball(radius=radius, center=center, color=color))
